# Graph Report - orqlet  (2026-11-23)

## Corpus Check
- Large corpus: 1389 files · ~1,607,579 words. Semantic extraction will be expensive (many Claude tokens). Consider running on a subfolder.

## Summary
- 34482 nodes · 66546 edges · 2462 communities (2351 shown, 111 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6327 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output
